### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCPは「届いたかを確認しながら、順番どおり確実に届ける」通信方式で、UDPは「確認や順序保証をせず、速さを優先して送りっぱなしにする」通信方式です。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para actualizar tu rama local con los cambios de la principal y mantener un historial lineal, siempre que nadie más esté usando esa rama.
Usa merge cuando la rama ya es compartida o quieres conservar el historial tal cual ocurrió, porque reescribir commits que otros tienen causa conflictos.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是可靠的、有连接、按字节流顺序交付的；UDP 不保证送达和顺序，但开销小、速度快。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase when the branch is yours and still private, so you can tidy its commits into a clean history before anyone else builds on it. Merge when the branch is shared or already public, because rewriting it would break everyone else's copy.
```

### direct-zh-howto

```
--- step 1: user says: bun test 怎么只跑一个测试文件？
  [partner message] `bun test` 直接跟文件路径就行：

```bash
bun test tests/user.test.ts
```

它也接受文件名的一部分，比如 `bun test user` 会跑所有文件名里带 `user` 的测试。想只跑某一个测试用例，用 `-t`（或 `--test-name-pattern`）按名字过滤：

```bash
bun test tests/user.test.ts -t "登录失败"
```
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log is a file where a database first records every change it intends to make, before touching the actual data files. If the system crashes mid-write, it replays that log on restart to recover the changes it had promised to keep.
```

| | Mac mini M4 | MacBook Air M4 |
|---|---|---|
| Price | $599 | $999 |
| Memory | 16 GB | 16 GB |
| Power | Plugged in all the time, by design | Battery, which wears down if kept at full charge all the time |
| Lid or display | No lid, no screen needed | Runs with the lid closed, but only while on power |
| Wired network | Gigabit Ethernet built in | Wi-Fi only, needs a USB-C adapter for wired |
| Location | Stays at home | Goes wherever you go |

The Air only makes sense if you want me with you on the road. Its battery is a bonus there, not something you need at home.

--- step 1: user answers the open decision: go
  [partner message] 做好了，周五京沪高铁的看板已经填进了现在的票价:G1、G3 都是 553 元,600 以下 2 班。之后每天的查询结果会自动更新上去。
  [an app card under the message: made "周五京沪高铁票价", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 2229 characters): <main class="k-app">
<div class="k-sum">
  <div class="k-sum-label">周五 北京→上海 · 600 元以下</div>
  <div class="k-sum-value" id="cnt">—</div>
  <div class="k-sum-sub" id="low">还没看</div>
</div>
<h2 class="k-h">班次与票价</h2>
<div class="k-card">
  <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
</div>
<script>
(function () {
  var LIMIT = 600;
  var list = document.getElementById('list');
  var cnt = document.getElementById('cnt');
  var low = document.getElementById('low');

  function esc(s) {
    return String(s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) {
      return { '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c];
    });
  }

  function draw(v) {
    var trains = (v && v.trains) || [];
    if (!trains.length) {
      list.innerHTML = '<div class="k-empty">还没有票价，看完会列在这里。</div>';
      cnt.textContent = '—';
      low.textContent = '还没看';
      return;
    }
    var under = trains.filter(function (t) { return t.price < LIMIT; });
    cnt.textContent = under.length + ' 班';
    if (under.length) {
      var min = Math.min.apply(null, under.map(function (t) { return t.price; }));
      low.textContent = '最低 ¥' + min + ' · 第 ' + (v.day || '?') + ' 天查的';
    } else {
      low.textContent = '暂时没有 600 元以下的 · 第 ' + (v.day || '?') + ' 天查的';
    }
    list.innerHTML = trains.map(function (t) {
      var ok = t.price < LIMIT;
      var sub = t.seats != null ? '二等座余 ' + t.seats + ' 张' : '余票未查';
      return '<div class="k-row" style="' + (ok ? '' : 'opacity:.5') + '">' +
        '<div class="k-row-main">' + esc(t.no) + (ok ? ' · 600 以下' : '') + '</div>' +
        '<div class="k-row-sub">' + esc(sub) + '</div>' +
        '<div class="k-row-value" style="color:' + (ok ? 'var(--d-good)' : 'var(--d-muted)') + '">¥' + esc(t.price) + '</div>' +
        '</div>';
    }).join('');
  }

  Promise.resolve(ducter.get('trains')).then(function (v) {
    Promise.resolve(ducter.get('day')).then(function (d) {
      draw(Object.assign({}, v, { day: d }));
    });
  });
  ducter.onChange(function () {
    Promise.resolve(ducter.get('trains')).then(function (v) {
      Promise.resolve(ducter.get('day')).then(function (d) {
        draw(Object.assign({}, v, { day: d }));
      });
    });
  });
})();
</script>
</main>; its data now (what it opens with): {"trains":[{"no":"G1","price":553,"seats":12},{"no":"G3","price":553,"seats":4},{"no":"G7","price":626,"seats":null}],"day":3}

  [an app card under the message: made "hawdb nightly writes", with Open] the app's file now (v2, 1637 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <div class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">Latest night</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="latest">—</div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="latest-sub">Nightly hawdb write benchmark</div>
  </div>
  <h2 class="k-h">Nights</h2>
  <div class="k-card">
    <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
    <div class="k-empty" id="empty">No runs yet. The benchmark reports here each night.</div>
  </div>
</main>
<script>
(function () {
  function fmt(n) {
    return n >= 1000 ? Math.round(n / 1000) + 'k' : String(n);
  }
  function render(v) {
    var nights = (v && v.nights) ? v.nights.slice() : [];
    var list = document.getElementById('list');
    var empty = document.getElementById('empty');
    list.innerHTML = '';
    if (!nights.length) {
      empty.style.display = '';
      document.getElementById('latest').textContent = '—';
      return;
    }
    empty.style.display = 'none';
    nights.reverse();
    var last = nights[0];
    document.getElementById('latest').textContent = fmt(last.writes) + ' writes/s';
    document.getElementById('latest-sub').textContent =
      'Night ' + last.night + ' · p99 ' + last.p99 + ' ms · compaction stalls ' + last.stalls;
    nights.forEach(function (n) {
      var row = document.createElement('div');
      row.className = 'k-row';
      row.innerHTML =
        '<div class="k-row-main">Night ' + n.night + '</div>' +
        '<div class="k-row-sub">p99 ' + n.p99 + ' ms · stalls ' + n.stalls + '</div>' +
        '<div class="k-row-value">' + fmt(n.writes) + '/s</div>';
      list.appendChild(row);
    });
  }
  ducter.onChange(render);
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"nights":[{"night":2,"writes":181000,"p99":4,"stalls":0},{"night":3,"writes":182000,"p99":4.1,"stalls":0}]}

  async function save() {

    if (!(amount > 0)) { $('amt').focus(); return; }

      id: Date.now(),
      amount: Math.round(amount * 100) / 100,
      cat,
      note: $('note').value.trim(),
      date: today()

    items = [entry, ...items];

    $('amt').value = '';
    $('note').value = '';
    $('add') && ($('add').open = false);
    document.querySelector('.k-add').open = false;
    render();
  }

  async function remove(id) {
    items = items.filter(e => e.id !== id);
    openId = null;
    await ducter.set('expenses', items);
    render();
  }

  async function load() {
    items = (await ducter.get('expenses')) || [];
    render();
  }

  $('save').onclick = save;
  renderCats();
  ducter.onChange(load);

    if (!/^\d{1,2}:\d{2}$/.test(t) || !(ml > 0)) return;
    var parts = t.split(':');

    feeds.push({ d: today(), t: t, ml: ml });

<div class="k-sum-label">Still waiting</div>
<div class="k-sum-value" id="count">4</div>
<div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">of 4 PRs · tick each one as it lands</div>
</section>
<h2 class="k-h">My PRs</h2>
<div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
<style>
.pr{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px;width:100%;text-align:left;background:var(--d-card);color:var(--d-ink);border:1px solid var(--d-line);border-radius:12px;padding:12px;margin-bottom:8px;font:inherit;cursor:pointer}
.box{width:22px;height:22px;border-radius:6px;border:2px solid var(--d-muted);flex:none;display:grid;place-items:center;font-size:14px;color:var(--d-bg)}
.pr[aria-pressed="true"] .box{background:var(--d-good);border-color:var(--d-good)}

</style>
<script>
var PRS=ducter.get("prs") || [];
var landed={};
function paint(){
  var list=document.getElementById('list');
  list.innerHTML='';
  PRS.forEach(function(p){
    var on=!!landed[p.id];
    var b=document.createElement('button');
    b.className='pr';
    b.setAttribute('aria-pressed',on?'true':'false');
    b.innerHTML='<span class="box"></span><span class="main"><div class="t"></div><div class="s"></div></span>';
    b.querySelector('.box').textContent=on?'✓':'';
    b.querySelector('.t').textContent=p.ref+' · '+p.title;
    b.querySelector('.s').textContent=on?'Landed':p.wait;
    b.onclick=function(){
      if(landed[p.id]){delete landed[p.id];}else{landed[p.id]=true;}
      ducter.set('landed',landed);
      paint();
    };
    list.appendChild(b);
  });
  var left=PRS.filter(function(p){return !landed[p.id];}).length;
  document.getElementById('count').textContent=left;
  document.getElementById('sub').textContent='of '+PRS.length+' still open · tick each as it lands';
  ducter.summary(left+' waiting');
}
function load(){
  Promise.resolve(ducter.get('landed')).then(function(v){
    landed=(v&&typeof v==='object')?v:{};
    paint();
  });
}
ducter.onChange(load);
load();
</script>
</main>; its data now (what it opens with): {"prs":[{"id":"d412","ref":"wey-gu/ducter#412","title":"Compaction cut","wait":"Waiting on Weizhen's review since Monday"},{"id":"m10287","ref":"nowledge-co/mem#10287","title":"Retry on 502","wait":"On CI, failing"},{"id":"h1592","ref":"nowledge-co/hawdb#1592","title":"Flaky compaction test","wait":"Waiting on Alice since Tuesday"},{"id":"d418","ref":"wey-gu/ducter#418","title":"Apps bar","wait":"Draft"}]}
  [also used tools] app
```

<div class="k-sum"><div class="k-sum-label">出差花销合计</div><div class="k-sum-value" id="total"><span class="k-none">还没记</span></div><div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">按出差和分类记</div></div>
<h2 class="k-h">花销明细</h2>
<div class="k-card"><div class="k-list" id="list"></div></div>
<details class="k-add"><summary>记一笔</summary>
<div class="k-field"><label>出差名称</label><input class="k-input" id="trip" placeholder="如 北京出差"></div>
<div class="k-field"><label>日期</label><input class="k-input" id="day" placeholder="如 11/20"></div>
<div class="k-chips" id="cats"><button class="k-chip" aria-pressed="true" data-c="交通">交通</button><button class="k-chip" aria-pressed="false" data-c="住宿">住宿</button></div>
<div class="k-field"><label>金额</label><input class="k-num" id="amt" inputmode="decimal"></div>
<button class="k-btn primary" id="save">保存</button>
</details>
</main>
<script>
let cat="交通";
document.querySelectorAll("#cats .k-chip").forEach(b=>{b.onclick=()=>{cat=b.dataset.c;document.querySelectorAll("#cats .k-chip").forEach(o=>o.setAttribute("aria-pressed",o===b?"true":"false"))}});
function draw(){
  const xs=ducter.get("items")||[];
  const total=xs.reduce((s,x)=>s+(+x.amt||0),0);
  co … getElementById("sub").textContent=xs.length?xs.length+" 笔":"按出差和分类记";
  const list=document.getElementById("list");
  list.textContent="";
  if(!xs.length){const e=document.createElement("p");e.className="k-empty";e.textContent="这里会列出每一笔花销";list.appendChild(e);return}
  xs.forEach(x=>{
    const r=document.createElement("div");r.className="k-row";
    const m=document.createElement("div");m.className="k-row-main";m.textContent=(x.trip||"出差")+" · "+(x.cat||"");
    const s=document.createElement("div");s.className="k-row-sub";s.textContent=[x.day,x.note].filter(Boolean).join(" · ");
    const v=document.createElement("div");v.className="k-row-value";v.textContent="¥"+(+x.amt||0).toLocaleString("en-US");
    r.append(m,s,v);list.appendChild(r);
  });
  ducter.summary(xs.length+" 笔,合计 ¥"+total.toLocaleString("en-US"));
}
document.getElementById("save").onclick=()=>{
  const amt=document.getElementById("amt").value;
  if(!amt)return;
  const xs=ducter.get("items")||[];
  xs.push({trip:document.getElementById("trip").value||"出差",day:document.getElementById("day").value,cat:cat,amt:+amt});
  ducter.set("items",xs);
  document.getElementById("amt").value="";
  draw();
};
draw();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"items":[{"trip":"北京出差","day":"11/20","cat":"交通","amt":662,"note":"G1 07:00 上海虹桥→北京南"},{"trip":"北京出差","day":"11/20–21","cat":"住宿","amt":1036,"note":"亚朵酒店（望京）两晚"}]}
